KaliBench: A Fine-Grained Benchmark for Cybersecurity Tool Use on Kali Linux with Runtime-Free Verifiable Rewards
KaliBench：面向Kali Linux上网络安全工具使用的细粒度基准测试与无运行时可验证奖励
LLMs are increasingly applied to cybersecurity workflows, where they are expected to translate analysts' intent into tool invocations. However, existing evaluations focus on knowledge-based assessments or end-to-end agentic tasks, and do not directly measure LLMs' ability to generate executable commands for real-world cybersecurity tools. This gap is critical because cybersecurity operations rely on strict command-line interfaces (CLIs), where minor syntax errors, incorrect flag--value bindings, or argument misordering can invalidate execution. We introduce KaliBench, a fine-grained benchmark and dataset for natural-language--to--CLI translation on Kali Linux, comprising 8,504 query--command pairs spanning 1,642 tools across 23 capability dimensions and 5 security phases. KaliBench is constructed via a manuscript-grounded pipeline with deterministic canonicalization and alias-aware evaluation, enabling precise and reproducible assessment of tool selection and argument construction. To ensure both semantic correctness and practical executability, we develop a multi-stage verification pipeline that combines LLM-based validation, sandboxed terminal execution, and human-in-the-loop refinement. Building on these fine-grained, deterministic signals, KaliBench further enables runtime-free verifiable rewards for training. Across three evaluation modes and 24 configurations of general-purpose and security-focused open-weight models, no open-weight model exceeds 42% exact-command accuracy in the unrestricted setting, highlighting the difficulty of accurate CLI-based cybersecurity tool use without explicit tool hints. We further show that supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards derived from KaliBench significantly improve an 8B model and achieve performance comparable to a 685B MoE model.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLM）在网络安全工作流中的应用日益广泛，期望其能将分析师的意图转化为实际工具调用。然而，现有评估主要集中在基于知识的测评或端到端智能体任务，未能直接衡量LLM生成真实网络安全工具可执行命令的能力。这一差距至关重要，因为网络安全操作依赖严格的命令行接口（CLI），微小的语法错误、错误的标志-值绑定或参数顺序错误都可能导致执行失败。我们提出了KaliBench，一个针对Kali Linux上自然语言到CLI翻译的细粒度基准和数据集，包含8,504个查询-命令对，覆盖23个能力维度和5个安全阶段中的1,642种工具。KaliBench通过基于文档的管道构建，采用确定性规范化和别名感知评估，实现对工具选择和参数构造的精确、可复现评估。为确保语义正确性和实际可执行性，我们开发了多阶段验证流程，结合基于LLM的验证、沙盒终端执行和人工介入优化。基于这些细粒度、确定性的信号，KaliBench进一步支持无运行时的可验证奖励用于训练。在三种评估模式和24种通用及安全导向的开源模型配置下，没有任何开源模型在无限制设置中超过42%的精确命令准确率，凸显了在缺乏显式工具提示的情况下准确使用CLI型网络安全工具的难度。我们还表明，使用来自KaliBench的可验证奖励进行监督微调和强化学习，显著提升了8B模型性能，并达到与685B MoE模型相当的水平。
👤 Pengfei Li, Naufal Suryanto, Sicheng Zhang, Muzammal Naseer 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv

Keyword Harnesses Fail Open: A Cheap Diagnostic Ladder for Tool-Use Claims in Small Language Models
关键词捕获机制会失效开放：小型语言模型工具使用声明的廉价诊断阶梯
Keyword-matching benchmarks can credit small models for tool use they never perform. We document such a false positive in a matched-architecture pair of Spanish security language models and propose a ladder of strict, cheap diagnostics. A 661.6M parameter model (approx. 65% code/technical text; no dedicated SFT) and a 1,109M model (web-heavy multi-phase curriculum; 6B-token tool-SFT) share decoder, tokenizer, and special tokens, scoring almost identically on lenient tool-use metrics (B4: 0.660 vs. 0.650). Verbatim-reproduction checks on training examples separate them completely: the 600M emits valid tool calls with generalized arguments on 6/6 examples; the 1B does so on 0/6 across checkpoints. A first-token probe localizes the 1B's failure to a missing prior (prob. $10^{-4}$--$10^{-5}$ on <|tool_call|>), which was erased by its web-heavy training phase. A targeted SFT recipe (diverse corpus, 5x higher learning rate, 2,202 steps, ~3.3 GPU-hours) repairs the 1B using three orders of magnitude fewer tokens than the failed phase. On all 269 corpus rows, valid emission rises from 0.100 to 0.959 (600M: 0.926). On 238 unseen prompts, the repaired 1B passes 0.536 vs. the 600M's 0.428 ($p = 0.004$). Embedding-drift checks show the repair did not move the trigger token's tied embedding (97.7% of the bf16 table remains bit-identical), meaning changes live in the surrounding network. Both models over-trigger, rarely answering negative prompts without a call (0.09 for 600M, 0.17 for repaired 1B). Factorial analyses confirm all repair configurations install the format, though suppression benefits from a diverse corpus remain a hypothesis due to seed sensitivity. This cheap diagnostic ladder costs minutes of CPU time and should gate tool-use claims on small models.
中文翻译
关键词匹配基准可能错误地将从未真正执行工具使用的模型归功于工具使用。我们记录了一个具有匹配架构的西班牙语安全语言模型对中的虚假阳性案例，并提出一套严格且低成本的诊断阶梯。一个661.6M参数模型（约65%代码/技术文本；无专用SFT）和一个1,109M模型（以网络为主、多阶段课程训练；6B令牌工具SFT）共享解码器、分词器和特殊标记，在宽松的工具使用指标上得分几乎相同（B4：0.660 vs. 0.650）。对训练示例的逐字重现检查将两者完全区分开：600M模型在6个示例中均发出带有泛化参数的有效工具调用；而1B模型在所有检查点上均为0/6。首次词元探测定位到1B模型的失败源于缺失先验（<|tool_call|>的概率为$10^{-4}$–$10^{-5}$），这由其以网络为主的训练阶段抹除。一种针对性的SFT配方（多样化语料库、5倍更高学习率、2,202步、约3.3 GPU小时）以比失败阶段少三个数量级的令牌修复了1B模型。在全部269个语料行上，有效发射率从0.100提升至0.959（600M：0.926）。在238个未见提示上，修复后的1B模型通过率为0.536，优于600M的0.428（p = 0.004）。嵌入漂移检查显示修复未改变触发标记的绑定嵌入（bf16表中97.7%保持位相同），意味着变化存在于周围网络中。两个模型均过度触发，很少在无调用需求时回答否定提示（600M：0.09，修复后1B：0.17）。因子分析确认所有修复配置均安装了格式，尽管多样语料带来的抑制优势因种子敏感性仍为假设。这一廉价诊断阶梯仅需几分钟CPU时间，应作为小型模型工具使用声明的门槛。
👤 Juan S. Santillana 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv
[2610.02122]

Argo-Bench: Evaluating Data Agents on Enterprise-Scale Workflows
Argo-Bench：在企业级工作流中评估数据智能体
Real-world enterprise data science and analytics workflows require reasoning across dozens of tables, performing statistical analyses, and acting on the results. Established text-to-SQL benchmarks evaluate query generation alone, and audits have found their answer keys frequently wrong. Because real enterprise warehouses are too sensitive to release, these benchmarks are built on public datasets where a business event fits in a single table. We introduce Argo-Bench, an evaluation framework comprising 210 data science and analytics tasks. Drawing on public data, peer-reviewed industry literature, and regulatory filings, we simulate a food delivery platform in New York City at true scale, with 81 million orders in 2024, grounded economics, fraud patterns, and marketplace incentives. We export this world to an ERP warehouse of 235 tables and 7.5 billion rows, modeled on the Oracle E-Business Suite schema. The simulator's ground-truth state is withheld from the warehouse the agent sees, so tasks require reconstructing facts by navigating the warehouse before acting on them. Argo-Bench goes beyond text-to-SQL: the agent files actions such as banning fraudulent accounts, allocating courier incentive budgets, or issuing back pay, and the grader scores each by its consequences in the simulator. Every task has an executable reference solution that demonstrates solvability using only the warehouse. The strongest of 14 frontier and open-weight models scores 95 or higher on only 34.8% of tasks and averages 59.5 points. We hope Argo-Bench drives progress toward agents that understand, navigate, and act within real data environments.
中文翻译
真实世界的企业数据科学与分析工作流需要跨数十张表格进行推理、执行统计分析并根据结果采取行动。现有的文本转SQL基准测试仅评估查询生成，且审计发现其答案键经常错误。由于真实企业数据仓库过于敏感无法公开，这些基准测试基于公共数据集构建，其中商业事件仅限于单张表。我们提出Argo-Bench，一个包含210个数据科学与分析任务的评估框架。基于公共数据、同行评审行业文献和监管文件，我们模拟了纽约市一家真实的食品配送平台，规模达到2024年8100万订单，具备真实经济背景、欺诈模式和市场激励机制。我们将该系统导出为包含235张表和75亿行数据的ERP仓库，其结构参照Oracle E-Business Suite模式。模拟器的真实状态对智能体可见的仓库是隐藏的，因此任务要求智能体通过导航仓库重建事实后才能采取行动。Argo-Bench超越了文本转SQL：智能体可执行如封禁欺诈账户、分配骑手激励预算或发放补薪等操作，评分器根据其在模拟器中的后果进行打分。每个任务都有可执行的参考解决方案，证明仅使用仓库即可求解。14个前沿及开源模型中最强者仅在34.8%的任务中得分达到95或以上，平均分为59.5分。我们希望Argo-Bench能推动智能体在真实数据环境中理解、导航并行动的能力进步。
👤 Gabriel Tomitsuka, Arman Raayatsanati, Emma Xing, Duke Gand, Joseph J Ma 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv
[2610.01592]

Which LLM to pick? Online Active Model Selection for Large Language Models
如何选择大语言模型？面向大语言模型的在线主动模型选择
Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to process streaming data, with practitioners relying on benchmarks to select the best model even though these signals only approximate real performance. While oracle annotations can provide reliable feedback, they are often costly and difficult to obtain at scale. To address this challenge, we propose ONLINE LLM PICKER, the first framework for active model selection for LLMs in online settings. Given an arbitrary stream of queries and a limited annotation budget, ONLINE LLM PICKER selects the most informative prompts for annotation to identify the best LLM among candidate models. Across multiple tasks including 10 datasets, for over 130 language models, we show that ONLINE LLM PICKER saves annotation cost by up to 71.67% while reliably identifying the best or near-best model for the stream. We also show that using the returned model for sequential generation on unannotated prompts across the stream reduces regret by up to a factor of 2.51x, indicating that ONLINE LLM PICKER can identify the best or near-best model well before processing all streaming prompts.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）正越来越多地应用于流式数据处理，从业者依赖基准测试来选择最优模型，但这些指标仅近似反映真实性能。虽然理想标注可提供可靠反馈，但其成本高昂且难以规模化获取。为应对这一挑战，我们提出ONLINE LLM PICKER，这是首个面向在线场景的大语言模型主动模型选择框架。给定任意查询流和有限标注预算，ONLINE LLM PICKER选择最具信息量的提示进行标注，以识别候选模型中的最佳者。在涵盖10个数据集、超过130个语言模型的多任务实验中，ONLINE LLM PICKER最多可节省71.67%的标注成本，同时可靠地识别出流中表现最佳或接近最佳的模型。我们还发现，使用返回的最佳模型对未标注提示进行连续生成，可使遗憾度降低至原来的1/2.51，表明ONLINE LLM PICKER能在处理全部流式提示前，就准确识别出最优或近优模型。
👤 Alessandro Turrin, Patrik Okanovic, Torsten Hoefler, Nezihe Merve Gürel 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv
[2610.01491]
agent-benchmarkagent-memory
Auditing Web Agent Evaluation on WebArena-Lite: Human Review of Outcomes and Trajectories
对WebArena-Lite中网络智能体评估的审计：人工对结果与轨迹的审查
Web agents are an important application of large language models, yet their evaluation often depends on rule based or language model evaluators that inspect only the final outcome. Human verification of task completion and detailed analysis of failed trajectories remain limited. We audit all 165 WebArena Lite tasks under six evaluation conditions built from GPT 5.5 and an untrained Qwen3.5 9B model. The audit retains the original score, corrects false negatives from the automatic evaluator, identifies the first consequential error, and examines progress across the trajectory. We also study a Memory and Analysis Support Mechanism (MASM), which maintains explicit execution state, and Guide Text, which provides task relevant procedural guidance. Across four GPT 5.5 settings, human review recovers 5.45 to 8.49 percentage points of success missed by the evaluator. With a 25 step budget, Guide Text raises corrected success with MASM from 34.55% to 38.18%. On the untrained Qwen3.5 9B model, MASM raises the evaluator score from 13.90% to 18.80%. Review of 102 failed GPT 5.5 trajectories reveals frequent scrolling loops, unfinished exploration, premature answers, invalid actions, and incomplete form workflows. Step level evidence further shows that substantial early progress can coexist with a final failure. These results show why final scores alone provide an incomplete account of web agent behavior and motivate human grounded, trajectory aware verification.
中文翻译
网络智能体是大语言模型的重要应用，但其评估往往依赖基于规则或语言模型的评估器，仅检查最终结果。人工对任务完成情况的验证以及对失败轨迹的详细分析仍然有限。我们对WebArena Lite中的全部165个任务在六个由GPT 5.5和未经训练的Qwen3.5 9B模型构建的评估条件下进行了审计。审计保留了原始分数，纠正了自动评估器的误判，识别出首个关键错误，并分析了整个轨迹中的进展。我们还研究了记忆与分析支持机制（MASM），该机制维护显式的执行状态，以及引导文本（Guide Text），提供与任务相关的程序指导。在四种GPT 5.5设置下，人工审查恢复了5.45至8.49个百分点的成功率损失。在25步预算下，引导文本使MASM修正后的成功率从34.55%提升至38.18%。在未经训练的Qwen3.5 9B模型上，MASM将评估器得分从13.90%提升至18.80%。对102条失败的GPT 5.5轨迹的人工审查揭示了频繁的滚动循环、未完成的探索、过早回答、无效操作和不完整的表单流程。步骤级证据进一步表明，大量早期进展可与最终失败共存。这些结果说明，仅凭最终得分无法完整描述网络智能体的行为，强调了基于人工、轨迹感知的验证的重要性。
👤 Chengguang Gan, Zimeng He, Yoshihiro Tsujii, Ken-ichiro Kobayashi, Hiroki Itoh et al. 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv
[2610.01471]
agent-benchmarkagentic-rl
When Does a Second Model Help? Cross-Model Review in LLM Verification
第二个模型何时有帮助？大型语言模型验证中的跨模型评审
Large language models now generate code, documentation, and analyses, and are increasingly used to review such output. We ask when a second review by a different model helps. Building on the author's earlier preprints, which varied context, repetition, and role structure within one model, we test model independence in a controlled experiment: 30 artifacts with 150 planted errors, 10 review conditions, and 900 review sessions with three reviewer models from two developers. In this experiment, (1) a top-tier cross-model reviewer is not significantly different in F1 from same-model review in a fresh session (CCR), which does not establish equivalence; (2) the two find partly different errors (Jaccard 41.2%); and (3) at two review calls, one CCR plus one cross-model review matches more planted errors than two CCR reviews (56.7% vs. 42.7%; Holm-adjusted p=.006), but not significantly more than two reviews by the top-tier cross-model reviewer, so model difference and reviewer capability are not separated. A lightweight cross-model reviewer scores no higher than same-model review. Withholding requirements from the reviewer raises F1 for the two lower tiers but not the top tier, in untested point estimates whose pattern depends on how failed sessions are scored. Before analysis we audited all session records, excluding one baseline run of uncertain provenance and 14 failed calls; results with all sessions are also reported. A partial check on public detector outputs from another benchmark neither replicates nor contradicts the main comparison. Records, artifacts, and scripts are available from the author on request.
中文翻译
大型语言模型现在能够生成代码、文档和分析，并被越来越多地用于审查此类输出。我们探讨了由不同模型进行第二次评审在什么情况下有助于提升质量。基于作者早期的预印本，这些研究调整了同一模型内的上下文、重复次数和角色结构，我们在受控实验中测试了模型独立性：包含150个故意植入错误的30个样本，10种评审条件，以及来自两名开发者的三名评审模型共900次评审会话。实验结果表明：（1）顶级跨模型评审者在F1指标上与全新会话中的同模型评审（CCR）无显著差异，但未确立等效性；（2）两者发现的部分错误不同（Jaccard相似度为41.2%）；（3）在两次评审中，一次CCR加一次跨模型评审比两次CCR评审更匹配更多植入错误（56.7% vs. 42.7%；Holm校正p=0.006），但并未显著优于两次顶级跨模型评审，因此无法区分模型差异与评审能力的影响。轻量级跨模型评审的表现不高于同模型评审。向评审者隐藏要求可提高低层级模型的F1值，但对顶级模型无效，这一趋势依赖于失败会话的评分方式，且未经充分验证。分析前我们审计了所有会话记录，排除了一次来源不明的基线运行和14次失败调用；也报告了包含所有会话的结果。对另一基准公开检测器输出的初步检查既未复现也未反驳主要比较结果。数据、样本和脚本可应作者请求获取。
👤 Tae-Eun Song 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv
[2610.01428]

Generalization Is Stability, Not Accuracy: Multi-Axis Evaluation of LLMs
泛化即稳定性，而非准确性：大语言模型的多维度评估
Generalization in large language models (LLMs) is the ability to produce consistent and semantically stable outputs when the same input is expressed in different ways. Existing work typically evaluates generalization through aggregate accuracy on a single prompt format, task, or set of variations, which conflates robustness with overall benchmark performance. In this work, we show generalization evaluation at the level of individual examples, across multiple input variants, and across different aspects of model behavior, focusing on variability rather than reducing performance to a score that can be improved through narrow training or other ways that obfuscate generalization evaluation. Following this view, we introduce the Stability-Aware Generalization Objective (SAGO), a framework that measures how much model behavior changes for the same input under different variations and benchmarks, capturing variability across several dimensions including generation consistency, internal activations, confidence, and response mirroring. We show that many commonly used models exhibit statistically significant and consistent generalization instability: no model generalizes uniformly, behavioral axes capture independent failure modes, and cross-dataset variation can reverse model rankings.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）的泛化能力是指在相同输入以不同方式表达时，仍能产生一致且语义稳定的输出。现有研究通常通过单一提示格式、任务或变化集合上的平均准确率来评估泛化，这将鲁棒性与整体基准表现混为一谈。本文提出在个体示例层面、跨多种输入变体及模型行为的不同方面进行泛化评估，强调变异性的测量，而非将其简化为可通过窄训练或其他手段优化的单一得分。基于此，我们引入稳定性感知泛化目标（SAGO）框架，用于衡量同一输入在不同变体和基准下模型行为的变化程度，捕捉生成一致性、内部激活、置信度和响应镜像等多个维度的变异。我们发现，许多常用模型表现出统计上显著且一致的泛化不稳定性：没有模型实现均匀泛化，行为轴线捕获了独立的失败模式，跨数据集的变化甚至可能逆转模型排名。
👤 Nagham Omar, Mahmoud Jabarin, Maya Rozenshtein, Rom Himelstein, Avi Mendelson et al. 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv
[2610.01427]
agent-benchmark
SHAMS: An Audio-Grounded Pronunciation Benchmark for Levantine Arabic
SHAMS：面向黎凡特阿拉伯语的语音基础发音评测基准
Levantine Arabic (LA) is spoken by tens of millions of people, creating a pressing need for shared benchmarks to evaluate LA speech-language technologies. Evaluating such technology is particularly challenging given LA's internal diversity and its opaque and non-standardized orthography. We present SHAMS (SHami Annotated Multi-dialect Speech), a benchmark comprising 1,300 utterances drawn from open audio corpora, balanced across five LA varieties (Urban and Rural Palestinian, and Urban Jordanian, Lebanese, and Syrian). Each utterance is represented across four aligned tiers: audio, unvocalized orthography, diacritized text, and phonetic transcription. This structure supports evaluation of various downstream tasks such as diacritization, grapheme-to-phoneme conversion, automatic speech recognition, and audio-to-phoneme, grounded in audio and stratified by variety. We benchmark open and proprietary models across these tasks to demonstrate the utility of this benchmark for measuring progress across LA. We release SHAMS at https://shams-nlp.github.io .
中文翻译

Does AI-Generated Scientific Text Follow Human Argumentation Patterns? A CARS-Based Comparison of Research Article Introductions
AI生成的科学文本是否遵循人类论证模式？基于CARS模型的研究论文引言对比分析
Large language models are moving from helping write up research to helping do it, which makes it important to know how the scientific text they produce differs from human writing. Work on this question has stayed mostly at the surface, using lexical and stylistic cues that light paraphrasing erases. We look instead at rhetorical structure, the sequence of argumentative moves through which a text makes its case. We study research-article introductions under Swales' CARS model, and compare original introductions from published linguistics articles with generated counterparts of the same papers. We find that human-written introductions are more flexible in which moves they use and in what order, while the generated ones are more uniform. Giving the models the CARS definitions makes them more rigid.

ARCCS: An Automated Regulatory Compliance Checking System
ARCCS：一个自动合规检查系统
Regulatory compliance checking - deciding whether a target document satisfies the obligations of a regulation - requires interpreting dense legal text, identifying which provisions apply, and grounding each decision in explicit evidence. We present ARCCS, an end-to-end, automated, agentic, and regulation-agnostic Legal NLP system for compliance checking. ARCCS decomposes raw regulatory text into atomic, traceable requirements and evaluates a target document against them using retrieved evidence, confidence scores, and human-interpretable justifications. This design decouples compliance assessment from any fixed regulatory template or predefined rule set, enabling the pipeline to operate over regulations of varying size and structure. We evaluate ARCCS in two complementary settings. First, in a GDPR policy-document evaluation, LLM-based judges find its decisions and justifications legally and evidentially consistent in up to 96.67% of the assessed cases. Second, on an EU public-procurement benchmark comprising more than 1,200 individual rule checks, the system attains 98.8% accuracy in violation detection. ARCCS is, to our knowledge, the first fully open-source system for end-to-end regulatory compliance checking and auditable report generation.
中文翻译
合规检查——判断目标文档是否满足法规义务——需要解读密集的法律文本、识别适用条款，并将每个决策建立在明确证据之上。我们提出了ARCCS，一个端到端、自动化、代理式且与法规无关的法律自然语言处理系统，用于合规检查。ARCCS将原始法规文本分解为可追溯的原子性要求，并利用检索到的证据、置信度分数和人类可理解的论证来评估目标文档。这种设计使合规评估与任何固定法规模板或预定义规则集解耦，从而能够处理不同规模和结构的法规。我们在两个互补场景中评估了ARCCS：首先，在GDPR政策文档评估中，基于大模型的裁判发现其决策和论证在高达96.67%的案例中具有法律和证据一致性；其次，在包含超过1,200项独立规则检查的欧盟公共采购基准测试中，系统在违规检测方面达到了98.8%的准确率。据我们所知，ARCCS是首个完全开源的端到端合规检查与可审计报告生成系统。

What Wins a Vote? Formatting, Length, and Lexical Diversity in the French Compar:IA LLM Arena
什么赢得投票？法国Compar:IA LLM竞技场中的格式、长度与词汇多样性
LLM arenas turn pairwise human preferences into model rankings. Those preferences may reflect how an answer is presented as well as what it says. We take a stylometric approach to 137,293 decisive French-language votes from the July 2026 Compar:IA release; the primary formatting analysis includes 137,113 battles across 116 models, and the joint estimates use the 127,092 battles with all required measurements. For each battle, we reconstruct the response visible when the user voted. We then compare the raw ranking with rankings adjusted for formatting, length, readability, vocabulary variety, and sentence structure. Presentation is associated with winning, but length, bold text, and lists tend to occur together, making their individual contributions hard to separate. Across the measured features, two associations change least across specifications: bold usage (+11.0% win odds per standard deviation in the joint model) and moving-average type-token ratio (MATTR), a measure of vocabulary variety that is less sensitive to answer length (+16.8%). The bold association is substantially smaller in observed multi-turn conversations, whereas the MATTR association changes little; because users choose whether to continue, this difference is descriptive rather than causal. The full adjustment moves 36 of 116 models by at least ten ranks. Yet comparisons with external benchmarks do not show that adjusted rankings better measure capability. We therefore recommend publishing raw and adjusted rankings side by side as a transparent sensitivity analysis.
中文翻译
LLM竞技场将成对的人类偏好转化为模型排名。这些偏好可能反映答案的呈现方式，而不仅仅是内容本身。我们采用风格计量方法分析了2026年7月Compar:IA发布中137,293个决定性法语投票；主要格式分析涵盖137,113场对决，涉及116个模型，联合估计则使用了127,092场具备所有测量数据的对决。对于每一场对决，我们重建用户投票时可见的回答。随后，我们将原始排名与经过格式、长度、可读性、词汇多样性及句子结构调整后的排名进行比较。结果显示，呈现方式与胜出相关，但长度、加粗文本和列表常同时出现，难以分离其各自贡献。在所有测量特征中，两个关联在不同设定下变化最小：加粗使用（联合模型中每标准差胜率提高11.0%）和移动平均类型-词项比（MATTR），后者是衡量词汇多样性的指标，对答案长度不敏感（联合模型中每标准差胜率提高16.8%）。加粗效应在实际多轮对话中显著降低，而MATTR效应基本不变；由于用户自主决定是否继续，这一差异为描述性而非因果性。完整调整后，116个模型中有36个至少移动了十位排名。然而，与外部基准对比显示，调整后的排名并未更准确衡量模型能力。因此，我们建议以透明敏感性分析的方式并列发布原始与调整后的排名。

Science Utopia? Closed-Loop LLM Simulation of Academic Research Ecosystems
科学乌托邦？闭环LLM模拟学术研究生态系统
Scientific progress emerges from a longitudinal ecosystem in which researchers, institutions, funding agencies, collaboration networks, and the scientific literature co-evolve. As AI becomes increasingly involved throughout the scientific research cycle, understanding these interconnected and evolving processes becomes increasingly important. We introduce SciUtopia, a persistent, closed-loop LLM-agent simulation framework for studying academic research ecosystems. SciUtopia models interconnected scientific processes such as research-direction choice, collaboration, submission, peer review, resubmission, citation, funding, and researcher attrition, while maintaining evolving states across simulated years. Its configurable institutional mechanisms and information channels provide a controlled testbed for matched counterfactual experiments and targeted interventions. Across 61 simulation worlds, SciUtopia simulates over 40,000 researchers from 8,000 institutions, producing around 400,000 publication decisions and 1.2 million LLM-generated peer reviews. Using these longitudinal simulations, we find that rejection-driven resubmission substantially amplifies reviewer burden beyond population growth alone, cautious exploration balances citation impact with career success and long-term topic diversity, and resource inequality can emerge even without detectable cumulative advantage from narrowly winning early funding. Code is available at https://github.com/Ahren09/ScienceUtopia.

科学进步源自研究人员、机构、资助机构、合作网络与科学文献共同演化的长期生态系统。随着人工智能日益深度介入科研全流程，理解这些相互关联且持续演变的过程变得愈发重要。我们引入SciUtopia，一个持久的闭环LLM智能体模拟框架，用于研究学术研究生态系统。SciUtopia建模了包括研究方向选择、合作、投稿、同行评审、再提交、引用、资助及研究人员流失等相互关联的科学过程，并维持跨模拟年份的演化状态。其可配置的机构机制与信息通道提供了受控的试验环境，支持匹配的反事实实验与针对性干预。在61个模拟世界中，SciUtopia模拟了超过4万名研究人员来自8,000个机构，生成约40万次出版决策和120万条LLM生成的同行评审。利用这些纵向模拟，我们发现拒绝驱动的再提交显著放大了审稿人负担，远超人口增长本身的影响；谨慎探索在引文影响力、职业成功与长期主题多样性之间取得平衡；即使没有早期资助的明显累积优势，资源不平等也可能自发产生。代码已公开于https://github.com/Ahren09/ScienceUtopia。

Right Answers, Wrong States: Hidden Information Failures in Multi-Agent Collaboration
正确答案，错误状态：多智能体协作中的隐性信息失败
Multi-agent systems are often judged by whether they reach the correct answer. This can miss a distinct failure: collaboration may leave behind a corrupted information state even when the immediate decision is correct. We call this an off-query failure. To study this failure in collaborative decision support, we introduce OffQuery, which separately evaluates evidence verification (T1), shared-state reconstruction (T2), and task resolution (T3) in two representative high-stakes settings: healthcare and disaster response. Across GPT, Gemini, and Qwen models, standard collaboration shows much stronger task performance than state reliability. Averaged over 21 model--setting combinations, task resolution reaches 64.7%, while evidence verification and state reconstruction reach only 14.3% and 43.1%. We trace this gap to selective information use: current queries often bypass corrupted facts, which become consequential when later tasks require them. We further introduce ReGround, which resolves conflicting evidence, verifies shared facts, reconstructs a trusted state, and reasons over that state. Across seven models from three families, ReGround improves all three capabilities in every evaluated setting, with average relative gains of 309.0%, 82.9%, and 17.6% on T1, T2, and T3. Reliable collaboration therefore requires both a correct decision and a reliable shared state for future reasoning.
中文翻译
多智能体系统通常根据是否得出正确答案来评估。但这可能遗漏一种独特的问题：协作过程即使在即时决策正确的情况下，也可能留下被污染的信息状态。我们称这种现象为‘离查询失败’。为研究协作决策支持中的此类失败，我们提出了OffQuery，该框架在两个典型高风险场景——医疗和灾难响应中，分别评估证据验证（T1）、共享状态重建（T2）和任务解决（T3）。在GPT、Gemini和Qwen模型上，标准协作在任务表现上远强于状态可靠性。在21种模型-场景组合的平均结果中，任务解决率达到64.7%，而证据验证和状态重建仅分别为14.3%和43.1%。我们发现这一差距源于选择性使用信息：当前查询常绕过被污染的事实，但当后续任务需要这些事实时，其后果便显现。为此，我们进一步提出ReGround，它能解决冲突证据、验证共享事实、重建可信状态并基于该状态进行推理。在三个系列共七种模型上，ReGround在所有评估场景中均提升了三项能力，T1、T2和T3的平均相对提升分别为309.0%、82.9%和17.6%。因此，可靠的协作不仅需要正确决策，还需为未来推理提供可靠共享状态。

AGO AI Quality Gate: Evidence-First Release Decisions for Retrieval-Augmented Generation
AGO AI质量门：基于证据的检索增强生成发布决策
Enterprises adopting retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) face a recurring operational decision: promote, revise, or block a system version. The evidence is incomplete and the metrics come from fallible LLM judges. We report on AGO AI Quality Gate (AGO), an evidence-first quality-gate framework deployed in industrial RAG assessment engagements. AGO integrates four key components: a four-state decision model that treats missing data and judge errors as explicit outcomes; layered scoring combining deterministic checks, local guardrails, and structured LLM evaluation; a stratified beta-binomial gate that quantifies regression risk probabilistically; and a mandatory meta-evaluation protocol to validate the LLM judge before it influences decisions. Since engagement data is proprietary, we evaluate the judge layer on RAGBench, a public benchmark of 100k annotated RAG traces across 12 datasets. On identical stratified test samples (N=1200 per judge), a low-cost judge (gpt-4.1-nano) detects non-adherent answers barely above chance (AUROC 0.603 [0.570, 0.634]), despite producing flawless protocol output, while gpt-4o reaches 0.783 [0.756, 0.807] -- yet its per-domain performance still ranges from 0.62 to 0.88. A fixed-seed gate study spanning regression, no change, and improvement quantifies unsafe promotion, false-alarm cost, and improvement throughput. Under regression, the decision-grade profile reduces unsafe promotion to 22.2%-35.1%, against 29.3%-41.8% for a naive gate. These results support the design choices that judge quality must be measured per engagement and that point estimates alone are not a release decision.
中文翻译
企业在采用检索增强生成（RAG）时面临一个反复出现的操作决策：推动、修订或阻止系统版本。证据不完整，且评估指标来自不可靠的LLM评判者。我们报告了AGO AI质量门（AGO）——一个在工业级RAG评估中部署的以证据为先的质量门框架。AGO整合了四个核心组件：四状态决策模型，将缺失数据和评判错误明确视为结果；分层评分机制，结合确定性检查、本地护栏和结构化LLM评估；分层贝塔二项分布门，概率化量化回归风险；以及强制性的元评估协议，确保LLM评判者在影响决策前经过验证。由于参与数据为专有信息，我们基于公开的RAGBench基准（涵盖12个数据集的10万条标注RAG轨迹）评估评判层。在相同分层测试样本（每评判者N=1200）下，低成本评判者（gpt-4.1-nano）检测非合规回答的表现仅略高于随机水平（AUROC 0.603 [0.570, 0.634]），尽管其协议输出完美；而gpt-4o达到0.783 [0.756, 0.807]，但其各领域表现仍介于0.62至0.88之间。一项固定种子门研究覆盖回归、无变化和改进三种情形，量化了不安全发布、误报成本和改进吞吐量。在回归情况下，决策级配置将不安全发布降至22.2%-35.1%，低于朴素门的29.3%-41.8%。这些结果支持了关键设计选择：评判质量必须按项目衡量，且仅凭点估计不足以作为发布决策。

My FAULT: Self-Diagnosis as Credit Assignment in Self-Evolving Agentic Reinforcement Learning
My FAULT：自演化智能体强化学习中的自我诊断作为信用分配
Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful approach for training large language model agents on multi-step tasks, yet reliance on terminal outcome rewards creates two credit-assignment problems, particularly in long-horizon tasks. First, same-outcome rollout groups provide no learning signal from terminal rewards. Second, terminal rewards provide only trajectory-wide feedback, making it difficult to identify which decisions caused a failure. Recent work supplements terminal rewards with finer-grained information from trajectory analysis, such as natural-language reflections on intermediate decisions and errors. However, natural-language diagnoses are difficult to use directly for credit assignment: their error claims may be unreliable, and they do not quantify how much each error should affect learning. We propose Self-Diagnosis-guided Terminal Credit Redistribution (FAULT), which turns diagnosed errors into explicit step-level credit anchored by terminal outcomes. FAULT checks diagnostic evidence and learns relative error costs from task outcomes. During training, the policy and self-diagnoser co-evolve, while error costs are updated online from recent outcomes. On ALFWorld, FAULT recovers learning signals from same-outcome groups, reaching 95% signal coverage versus 41% for GRPO and 72% for GiGPO, while better localizing credit to specific error steps. Across two model scales, FAULT delivers strong. improvements on the long-horizon ALFWorld and WebShop tasks while remaining competitive on short-horizon Search-based QA.
中文翻译
智能体强化学习（RL）已成为训练大语言模型智能体完成多步任务的强大方法，但依赖终端结果奖励带来了两个信用分配难题，尤其在长时程任务中更为显著。首先，相同结果的轨迹组无法从终端奖励获得学习信号；其次，终端奖励仅提供全局轨迹反馈，难以定位导致失败的具体决策。近期工作通过轨迹分析引入更细粒度的信息，如对中间决策和错误的自然语言反思。然而，自然语言诊断难以直接用于信用分配：其错误声明可能不可靠，且无法量化每个错误应影响学习的程度。我们提出自诊断引导的终端信用重分配（FAULT），将诊断出的错误转化为以终端结果为锚点的显式步骤级信用。FAULT检验诊断证据，并从任务结果中学习相对错误成本。训练过程中，策略与自诊断器共同演化，同时根据近期结果在线更新错误成本。在ALFWorld任务中，FAULT从相同结果组中恢复了学习信号，信号覆盖率达95%（GRPO为41%，GiGPO为72%），并更精准地定位信用至特定错误步骤。在两种模型规模下，FAULT在长时程的ALFWorld和WebShop任务中均取得显著提升，同时在短时程基于搜索的问答任务中保持竞争力。
👤 Yihua Zhu, Qianying Liu, Weixu Qiao, Xuan Ren, Weiwei Xu et al. 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv

AgSpec: Pushing the Limits of Retrieval-Based Speculative Decoding in Coding Agent Pipelines
AgSpec：推动基于检索的推测解码在代码智能体流水线中的极限
Retrieval-based speculative decoding (SD) drafts tokens by copying continuations from existing text, which suits coding agents that repeatedly reproduce code, logs, and earlier attempts. Yet existing methods fall short in agent pipelines: much of the reusable text is missing from their corpora or stored in a form that differs from what the agent emits, and their draft lengths ignore that accept length varies across agents and drifts over turns. We present AgSpec, a framework that supplies the corpus and draft-length policies that existing retrieval engines lack in coding-agent pipelines. AgSpec retrieves from session, workspace, and global corpora, retaining the ongoing session trajectory and indexing opened files in the agent's emission format. It bounds each agent's draft length with an offline-profiled cap and adapts the length online from verification feedback. On two repository-level multi-agent coding benchmarks, AgSpec outperforms five retrieval-based drafters and EAGLE-3 in most evaluated settings, raising generation throughput over autoregressive decoding up to 4.37$\times$ at batch size 1 and 4.76$\times$ at batch size 16. AgSpec also remains effective on benchmarks without a repository or a multi-agent pipeline, showing that its gains generalize to coding agents broadly.
中文翻译
基于检索的推测解码（SD）通过复制已有文本的延续来草拟标记，这适用于重复复现代码、日志和早期尝试的代码智能体。然而，现有方法在智能体流水线中表现不足：大量可重用文本缺失于其语料库中，或以与智能体输出形式不同的方式存储；且其草稿长度未考虑不同智能体间接受长度的差异以及随轮次变化的漂移。我们提出AgSpec，一种为现有检索引擎在代码智能体流水线中所缺乏的语料库和草稿长度策略提供支持的框架。AgSpec从会话、工作区和全局语料库中检索，保留正在进行的会话轨迹，并以智能体输出格式索引已打开文件。它通过离线预配置的上限约束每个智能体的草稿长度，并根据验证反馈在线调整长度。在两个仓库级多智能体代码基准测试中，AgSpec在多数评估设置下优于五种基于检索的草稿生成器及EAGLE-3，生成吞吐量相比自回归解码最高提升4.37倍（批量大小为1）和4.76倍（批量大小为16）。AgSpec在无仓库或无多智能体流水线的基准上也保持有效性，表明其优势可广泛推广至各类代码智能体。
👤 Sumin Lee, Sukmin Cho, Suengjae Lim, Youngjin Kwon 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv

JoinGR: Learning to Traverse Join Graphs for Table Retrieval
JoinGR：学习遍历连接图以实现表格检索
Retrieving the right tables is a prerequisite for Text-to-SQL over realistic databases. Dense table retrievers rank schema elements independently, but this ignores a key source of evidence: some required tables are not mentioned in the question and become identifiable only through their join relationships to already relevant tables. We introduce JOINGR, a join-aware table retrieval method that treats the database join graph as the retrieval space. Columns are represented as graph nodes, while intra-table and foreign-key relationships are represented as typed edges. Given a question, JOINGR selects semantically similar anchor tables, traverses join edges with a query-conditioned scorer, and aggregates the resulting edge deposits into table scores. The scorer is a lightweight MLP on top of frozen query, node, and edge embeddings, trained with a pairwise margin loss over gold tables. On BIRD and Spider datasets, JOINGR is competitive with the strongest retrieval baselines. On BEAVER, a challenging enterprise benchmark with multi-hop table requirements, JOINGR substantially improves recall over dense retrieval and re-ranking baselines. Cross-domain experiments show that the learned scorer transfers across benchmarks, indicating that the method captures reusable joingraph traversal behavior.
中文翻译
在真实数据库上执行文本到SQL操作的前提是检索到正确的表格。密集表格检索器独立排名模式元素，但忽略了关键证据来源：某些必要表格在问题中并未提及，只有通过它们与已有相关表格的连接关系才能识别。我们提出JOINGR，一种面向连接关系的表格检索方法，将数据库连接图视为检索空间。列被表示为图节点，表内关系和外键关系则表示为带类型边。给定一个问题，JOINGR首先选择语义相似的锚定表格，然后使用查询条件评分器遍历连接边，并将所得边贡献聚合为表格得分。评分器是一个轻量级MLP，基于冻结的查询、节点和边嵌入，通过黄金表格上的成对边界损失进行训练。在BIRD和Spider数据集上，JOINGR与最强的检索基线相当。在BEAVER这一具有多跳表格需求的挑战性企业基准上，JOINGR显著提升了召回率，优于密集检索和重排序基线。跨域实验表明，学习到的评分器可在不同基准间迁移，说明该方法捕捉到了可复用的连接图遍历行为。
👤 Sandipan De, Abhijit Chakraborty, Sambaran Bandyopadhyay, Vivek Gupta 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv

LawCompass: Navigating from Legal QA to Multi-Agent Deep Research with Grounded Evidence
LawCompass：从法律问答到基于证据的多智能体深度研究的导航
Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) have significantly democratized access to legal information. Nevertheless, most existing legal assistants remain confined to multi-turn conversational QA, failing to support complex legal tasks that require systematic evidence retrieval, multi-step reasoning, and report-level synthesis. In this paper, we present LawCompass, an evidence-grounded legal assistant that navigates the transition from standard Legal QA to multi-agent deep research. LawCompass provides three task-oriented functions: Legal QA, which delivers precise, evidence-backed answers to legal questions; Professional Retrieval, which enables structured exploration of statutes and judicial cases via query rewriting; and Deep Research, which employs a multi-agent workflow to decompose complex legal tasks and synthesize comprehensive research reports. Crucially, LawCompass maintains explicit citation links across all modules, empowering users to directly verify system outputs against original legal sources. Evaluation results demonstrate that LawCompass provides a practical and scalable paradigm for transforming conversational AI into trustworthy and evidence-grounded legal research assistance.
中文翻译
大型语言模型（LLMs）和检索增强生成（RAG）的最新进展显著降低了获取法律信息的门槛。然而，大多数现有的法律助手仍局限于多轮对话式问答，无法支持需要系统性证据检索、多步推理和报告级综合的复杂法律任务。本文提出LawCompass，一种基于证据的法律助手，能够实现从标准法律问答到多智能体深度研究的过渡。LawCompass提供三种面向任务的功能：法律问答，为法律问题提供精准且有证据支持的答案；专业检索，通过查询重写实现对法规和司法判例的结构化探索；深度研究，采用多智能体工作流分解复杂法律任务并合成全面的研究报告。关键的是，LawCompass在所有模块中保持显式的引用链接，使用户能够直接验证系统输出与原始法律来源的一致性。评估结果表明，LawCompass为将对话式AI转化为可信且基于证据的法律研究辅助提供了一个实用且可扩展的范式。

It Takes Workflows to Evolve Better Workflows
构建更优工作流需要工作流本身
Tackling complex real-world tasks can exceed the capabilities of a single large language model (LLM), motivating the use of multi-agent workflows that coordinate specialized agents to work together on these tasks. Recent methods train LLMs to construct better workflows from execution outcomes, but they optimize only the workflow generator, while the other agents that build or execute each workflow remain fixed even though every outcome depends on all of them. However, extending training beyond the generator is challenging: the agents are coupled, and a workflow's outcome is a single sparse score that cannot tell which agent causes a failure. We propose FloWright, which leverages the workflow as a harness to optimize workflows. By introducing a hierarchical, structure-aware reward paradigm, FloWright enables one role to self-evolve and two or more roles to co-evolve, with no additional models, labels, or executions. Considering the limitation that workflows are commonly trained and evaluated on data that a single agent can already handle, we further propose DataWright, an adaptive data hardening approach that converts existing datasets into workflow-level tasks with increased difficulty. Across document, slide, chart, code, math, and finance tasks, small open models trained with FloWright achieve improved performance by up to $+7.41\%$, with co-evolving ($+5.03\%$) more roles gaining more than optimizing one of them alone ($+2.83\%$). Our project page: https://xhguo7.github.io/FloWright/.
中文翻译
解决复杂的现实世界任务可能超出单一大型语言模型（LLM）的能力，因此需要使用多智能体工作流，协调专门智能体协同完成这些任务。近期方法训练LLM从执行结果中构建更好的工作流，但仅优化工作流生成器，而其他构建或执行工作流的智能体则保持固定，尽管每个结果都依赖于所有智能体。然而，将训练扩展到生成器之外面临挑战：智能体之间存在耦合，且工作流的结果是一个单一的稀疏评分，无法判断是哪个智能体导致失败。我们提出FloWright，利用工作流作为框架来优化工作流。通过引入分层、结构感知的奖励范式，FloWright实现了单一角色的自我演化以及两个或更多角色的协同演化，无需额外模型、标签或执行。考虑到工作流通常在单个智能体即可处理的数据上训练和评估的局限性，我们进一步提出DataWright，一种自适应数据强化方法，将现有数据集转换为更具难度的工作流级任务。在文档、幻灯片、图表、代码、数学和金融任务中，使用FloWright训练的小型开源模型性能提升最高达+7.41%，其中协同演化（+5.03%）比单独优化一个角色（+2.83%）效果更好。项目页面：https://xhguo7.github.io/FloWright/。

A Citation-Grounded Benchmark for Trustworthy Earnings Call Transcript Analysis with Large Language Models
基于引文的基准测试：面向可信财报电话会议文本分析的大语言模型
Large language models (LLMs) have been increasingly used for financial document analysis, including earnings call transcripts (ECTs). Beyond generating standalone claims, users increasingly prefer grounded analyses that pair claims with verifiable citations from source documents to enable independent validation. However, evaluating such analytical claims typically requires extensive expert annotation, which is costly and difficult to scale, and real-world financial analysis commonly involves long context-question-answer triplets, further increasing task complexity. To address these challenges and benchmark the current landscape of grounded analysis by LLMs, we propose a numeric evidence evaluation method that enables groundedness assessment without reliance on expert annotation. We also introduce an automated dataset construction pipeline and construct ECTs-100 from the top 100 constituents of the S&P 500 to support benchmark of both groundedness and correctness. In addition, we examine conscious incompetence, a practical failure mode in financial analysis in which LLMs must detect when available evidence is insufficient and refrain from producing unsupported hallucinations. Empirical results show that LLMs perform well in groundedness but face notable limitations in correctness, with informational insufficiency presenting an additional challenge.
中文翻译
大语言模型（LLMs）在金融文档分析中的应用日益广泛，包括财报电话会议文本（ECTs）。用户不再满足于独立生成的声明，而是越来越倾向于基于可验证引文的分析，以便进行独立验证。然而，评估此类分析性声明通常需要大量专家标注，成本高昂且难以扩展；而现实中的金融分析常涉及长上下文-问题-答案三元组，进一步增加了任务复杂性。为应对这些挑战并基准化当前LLM在基于引文分析方面的表现，我们提出一种无需依赖专家标注的数值证据评估方法，用于评估分析的可追溯性。我们还引入自动化数据集构建流程，并基于标普500指数前100成分股构建了ECTs-100数据集，以支持对可追溯性和正确性的基准测试。此外，我们考察了‘有意识的无能’这一实际失败模式——即LLM必须在现有证据不足时识别并避免生成无依据的幻觉。实验结果表明，LLMs在可追溯性方面表现良好，但在正确性方面存在明显局限，信息不足构成了额外挑战。

Beyond Leaderboards: Tokenomics of Agentic Small Language Model Ensembles
超越排行榜：智能体小语言模型集成的代币经济学
As large language models (LLMs) move from standalone assistants into agentic workflows, evaluation must extend beyond scalar leaderboard accuracy to account for operational reliability, cost, latency, and token efficiency. We use an agentic ensemble of small language models (SLMs) with an SLM-judge-mediated feedback loop as a case study for such beyond-leaderboard evaluation. On the 541-prompt IFEval benchmark, the best ensemble achieves 97.34% strict prompt accuracy, exceeding the strongest standalone LLM baseline, gpt-5.4, by 5.81 percentage points while operating in a lower-cost regime. We then analyze the tokenomics and operational behavior behind this gain, including cost per sample, token composition, useful-output goodput, feedback-loop recovery, latency decomposition, and performance across instruction categories and constraint counts. Our results show that agentic SLM ensembles can trade additional test-time tokens and orchestration overhead for improved instruction-following fidelity, motivating multi-dimensional evaluation protocols for future agentic AI systems.
中文翻译
随着大语言模型从独立助手演变为智能体工作流，评估必须超越单一的排行榜准确率，纳入操作可靠性、成本、延迟和代币效率等因素。我们以一个小语言模型（SLMs）智能体集成体，结合SLM裁判驱动的反馈循环为例，探讨此类超越排行榜的评估。在包含541个提示的IFEval基准上，最佳集成体实现了97.34%的严格提示准确率，超过最强的独立LLM基线gpt-5.4达5.81个百分点，同时运行在更低的成本区间。随后我们分析了这一性能提升背后的代币经济学与操作行为，包括每样本成本、代币构成、有效输出吞吐量、反馈循环恢复能力、延迟分解，以及在不同指令类别和约束数量下的表现。结果表明，智能体SLM集成体可以通过增加测试时的代币消耗和编排开销，换取更高的指令遵循精度，从而推动未来智能体AI系统采用多维度评估协议。
👤 Alexei N. Skurikhin, Emily M. Taylor, Nathan A. DeBardeleben 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv

VERITYGATE: A Four-Gate Schema-Level Faithfulness Framework and Paired Benchmark for Grounded LLM Narrations over Structured Evidence
VERITYGATE：一种四门式层级忠实度框架及基于结构化证据的地面大语言模型叙述配对基准
Fluent LLM explanations may not follow the evidence from a structured system. We present VERITYGATE, a four-gate checker for declared evidence IDs, entities, numbers, and claim types. It checks a fixed schema; it does not verify every fact in the prose. At r=0 and r=1, we test 900 instances per setting (450 grounded-ungrounded pairs) with GPT-4o-mini, Llama-3.3-70B, and Claude Sonnet 4.6. Under this schema-level contract and before repair, 80.3% of mini claims and 47.9% of Sonnet claims fail. These are verifier rejection rates, not prose-hallucination rates. One repair pass raises claim survival from 19.7% to 28.0% for mini and from 52.1% to 54.3% for Sonnet. Verified claims per example change by +0.14 for mini, -0.71 for Llama, and -0.47 for Sonnet, so survival and output volume must be reported together. A second Sonnet pass gives no clear gain. At r=1, Gate 4 covers 97.0%, 98.7%, and 100% of failing claims for mini, Llama, and Sonnet. Small human studies support the rules but show gaps between schema checks and correct prose. A domain-specific GPT-4o judge test shows an order effect, so it is only a usefulness check. We release the code and data.
中文翻译
流畅的大语言模型解释可能不遵循来自结构化系统的证据。我们提出VERITYGATE，一个针对声明证据ID、实体、数值和主张类型的四门检查器。它检查固定模式，而非验证散文中的每一个事实。在r=0和r=1条件下，每种设置测试900个实例（450对有依据-无依据），使用GPT-4o-mini、Llama-3.3-70B和Claude Sonnet 4.6。在此层级契约下且未修复前，mini主张的80.3%和Sonnet主张的47.9%未能通过验证。这些是验证器拒绝率，而非散文幻觉率。一次修复后，mini主张存活率从19.7%提升至28.0%，Sonnet从52.1%提升至54.3%。每个示例的验证主张数量变化分别为：mini增加0.14，Llama减少0.71，Sonnet减少0.47，因此必须同时报告存活率和输出量。第二次Sonnet修复未带来明显增益。在r=1时，Gate 4分别覆盖了mini、Llama和Sonnet失败主张的97.0%、98.7%和100%。小型人工研究支持规则，但显示模式检查与正确散文之间存在差距。领域特定的GPT-4o裁判测试显示顺序效应，因此仅作为有用性检查。代码和数据已公开。

PhysicsMate: A Curriculum-Grounded Bengali Benchmark for Secondary Physics QA with Small-Model Adaptation
PhysicsMate：面向中学物理问答的小模型适配课程基础型孟加拉语基准
Bengali secondary education lacks curriculum-grounded benchmarks for STEM question-solving, and general-purpose language models struggle with the precise terminology, unit conventions, and derivations that physics problems demand. We introduce PhysicsMate, a benchmark of 1834 question-answer pairs built from the National Curriculum and Textbook Board (NCTB) Grade 9-10 physics syllabus and grounded in a multi-relational knowledge graph of 1760 nodes and 2600 edges across ten ontological types. We Low-Rank Adapt at 0.6B, 1.7B, and 4B parameters, with a unified recipe and demonstrate a significant increase in closed-book accuracy in all scales (+5.5, +15.0, and +23.3 percentage points). A node-type analysis shows that the most benefited by adaptation is the structured curricular knowledge, which consists of physical quantities and named laws, while the least benefited is the loosely specified entity-level knowledge. The 4B model has been adapted and quantized to a small offline binary that can be used for local inference in resource constrained environments and offers a viable path to curriculum aligned physics support in environments with limited connectivity and hardware.
中文翻译

Self-Evolving Coding Rules for AI Coding Agents
AI编程代理的自演化编码规则
The performance of AI coding agents is highly dependent on their underlying coding rules. However, existing coding rules are typically hand-crafted and fixed, making the process labor-intensive and often suboptimal. In this work, we propose RuleEvolve, a self-evolving framework for coding rules. RuleEvolve maintains a pool of candidate coding rules and iteratively improves them. In each iteration, it employs an LLM-powered mutator module to generate variants from existing candidates, and then uses a judge module to evaluate these variants and update the pool with the best-performing ones. Extensive evaluations across two coding-agent frameworks, four backbone LLMs, and three benchmarks demonstrate that RuleEvolve outperforms both manual engineering and existing prompt optimization baselines in terms of functional correctness of the generated code, code length, and/or generation cost (e.g., tokens used).
中文翻译
AI编程代理的性能高度依赖于其底层编码规则。然而，现有编码规则通常为手工设计且固定不变，导致过程耗时且常不理想。本文提出RuleEvolve，一种自演化编码规则框架。RuleEvolve维护一个候选编码规则池，并迭代改进。每轮中，它利用LLM驱动的变异模块从现有候选中生成变体，并通过评判模块评估这些变体，将表现最佳者更新至规则池。在两种编程代理框架、四种骨干LLM和三个基准上的广泛评估表明，RuleEvolve在生成代码的功能正确性、代码长度和/或生成成本（如使用的token数）方面，均优于人工工程和现有提示优化基线。
👤 Zhengyuan Jiang, Reachal Wang, Yuepeng Hu, Yupu Wang, Yuqi Jia et al. 📅 2026-09-30 🔗 arXiv
[2610.00606]

Where's Waldo? Query-language Preference under Cross-lingual Knowledge Disparities
“沃尔多在哪里？”——跨语言知识差异下的查询语言偏好研究
Large Language Models increasingly serve as interfaces for knowledge-intensive information seeking tasks across languages by synthesizing multilingual evidence. Prior work has shown that they often exhibit query-language preference -- the tendency to favor sources written in the language of the query -- but has largely examined this behavior in settings where equivalent knowledge is available across languages. However, this bias becomes consequential when sources in different languages provide incomplete or inconsistent accounts of the same fact, since the information users receive then depends on the sources a model selects to use. To characterize query-language preference under such cross-lingual knowledge disparities, we introduce Waldo, a multilingual Question-Answering (QA) benchmark constructed from Wikipedia. Waldo contains 12K QA pairs targeting knowledge gaps, where a fact is available in one language but absent in another, and knowledge conflicts, where language editions provide conflicting versions of the same fact. Evaluating eight models across five languages, we find that when one language edition merely lacks the relevant fact, models generally use evidence from the other language regardless of the query language. Under conflicting accounts, however, model responses strongly align with the document in the query language, causing semantically equivalent queries to elicit different accounts depending on the user's language. Finally, we explore two different approaches that could mitigate this preference under knowledge conflicts: a mechanistic intervention that ablates attention heads associated with query-language preference, and LoRA-based training, which reduces the preference gap by up to 61.5%.
中文翻译
大型语言模型越来越多地作为跨语言知识密集型信息检索任务的接口，通过整合多语言证据来响应查询。先前研究表明，这些模型常表现出查询语言偏好——倾向于选择与查询语言相同的来源，但以往研究大多在各语言间具备等效知识的背景下进行。然而，当不同语言的来源提供不完整或不一致的事实描述时，这种偏差便变得至关重要，因为用户所获得的信息取决于模型选择使用的来源。为刻画在跨语言知识差异下的查询语言偏好，我们引入Waldo，一个基于维基百科构建的多语言问答（QA）基准测试。Waldo包含12,000个QA对，涵盖知识缺口（某一事实仅存在于一种语言中）和知识冲突（不同语言版本对同一事实给出矛盾描述）。我们在五种语言上评估了八种模型，发现当某一语言版本仅缺少相关事实时，模型通常会使用其他语言的证据，不受查询语言影响；但在存在冲突的情况下，模型回应强烈偏向于查询语言文档，导致语义等价的查询因用户语言不同而产生不同回答。最后，我们探索了两种可能缓解此偏好的方法：一种是机制干预，通过消融与查询语言偏好相关的注意力头；另一种是基于LoRA的训练，可将偏好差距降低高达61.5%。

Assessing the Impact of Language Disparity on Multilingual Linguistic Ability in Large Language Models
语言差异对大语言模型多语言语言能力影响的评估
Claims about the grammatical competence of multilingual language models vary sharply with how competence is measured, yet the interaction between evaluation paradigm, post-training, and language resource availability has not been systematically examined. We evaluate base and post-trained models from six families on MultiBLiMP, a syntactic minimal-pair benchmark covering 101 languages, using four evaluation methods. We report three principal findings. First, post-training degrades grammatical competence, but the magnitude of this effect is reduced unevenly by model scale, while low-resource languages bear the highest cost. Second, post-trained models retain grammatical knowledge they cannot articulate through explicit prompting, yet this is measurable only in high-resource languages, because near-chance baselines in low-resource settings leave little knowledge to hide. Third, native-language prompting recovers otherwise hidden competence on low-resource languages, demonstrating that only high-resource languages can be probed directly from unprompted probabilities. We conclude that multilingual grammatical evaluation must adopt language-informed, multi-paradigm protocols to avoid systematically underestimating low-resource abilities.
中文翻译

EurekaBench: Measuring Agentic Ability to Discover New Scientific Insights
EurekaBench：衡量智能体发现新科学洞见的能力
When Isaac Newton discovered the law of gravitation, he did so through an iterative process of analyzing observed data such as planetary patterns, finding the underlying mechanisms by describing patterns in mathematical equations, and refining his theory against the Moon's orbit, revealing the startling insight that the same force governs both falling apples and orbiting planets. Would it be possible for AI agents to make similar discoveries? To measure this ability, we introduce EurekaBench, a cross-domain benchmark that tests AI agents' ability to conduct long-horizon experiments and discover mechanisms that explain observations. We evaluate these mechanisms by the scientific insights that can be derived from them. EurekaBench contains an expert-verified set of 26 long-horizon tasks across neuroscience, computer science, chemistry, astrophysics, geophysics, and plasma physics, with a total of 306 scientific insights that the discovered mechanisms are expected to support. Our evaluation framework tests three axes of scientific discovery: agents' ability to follow known scientific constraints, the predictive accuracy of the discovered mechanisms, and whether these mechanisms yield scientific insights or inform future research. Our results show that current AI agents often overly fixate on predictive accuracy optimization, surpassing human scientists, while falling substantially short in deriving scientific insights.
中文翻译
当艾萨克·牛顿发现万有引力定律时，他通过迭代过程分析观测数据（如行星运动模式），用数学方程描述规律，并通过月球轨道验证其理论，揭示了苹果下落与行星绕转由同一力驱动这一惊人洞见。人工智能代理是否也能做出类似发现？为衡量这种能力，我们提出了EurekaBench，一个跨领域的基准，用于测试AI代理进行长周期实验并发现解释观测现象机制的能力。我们通过这些机制能推导出的科学洞见来评估其有效性。EurekaBench包含26个跨学科的长周期任务，涵盖神经科学、计算机科学、化学、天体物理学、地球物理学和等离子体物理学，共306项预期支持的科学洞见。我们的评估框架测试科学发现的三个维度：代理遵循已知科学约束的能力、所发现机制的预测准确性，以及这些机制是否产生科学洞见或启发未来研究。结果表明，当前AI代理往往过度专注于预测准确性的优化，甚至超过人类科学家，但在推导科学洞见方面却显著不足。

DuplexSpeechBench-Document Grounding: Benchmarking Document Grounding and Hallucinations in Voice Agents
DuplexSpeechBench-文档定位：语音代理中文档定位与幻觉的基准测试
Voice agents enable low-latency, natural interaction, yet their ability to faithfully ground responses in external documents remains underexplored. We introduce DuplexSpeechBench-Document Grounding (DSB-DG), a benchmark for evaluating document grounding in voice agents across five professional domains. DSB-DG targets three failure modes: Context Saturation, which measures grounding under increasing document length; Grounding Decay, which measures retention of document facts across multi-turn dialogue; and Proactive Grounding, which evaluates whether context re-injection mitigates conversational drift. The benchmark contains 1,636 adversarially verified QA pairs from 50 documents covering five professional domains, and supports fully automatic evaluation of grounding accuracy, hallucination, and response latency. Across systems spanning cascaded, proprietary full-duplex and real-time, and open-weight speech2speech architectures, we find substantial differences in effective grounding capacity. While cascaded pipeline (ASR-LLM-TTS) achieves the highest grounding accuracy, Gemini-Live and GPT-Realtime closely trail behind. Open-weight systems exhibit distinct failure modes, most notably an abrupt context-capacity collapse and multi-turn grounding decay. More broadly, grounding fidelity degrades with context and conversational load, and failures frequently manifest as unsupported generations rather than abstention. We show that contextual grounding as a key unresolved challenge for reliable full-duplex voice agents.

语音代理实现了低延迟、自然的交互，但其在外部文档中忠实定位响应的能力仍缺乏深入探索。我们引入DuplexSpeechBench-文档定位（DSB-DG），一个用于评估语音代理在五个专业领域中文档定位能力的基准。DSB-DG针对三种失效模式：上下文饱和度，衡量文档长度增加时的定位能力；定位衰减，衡量多轮对话中对文档事实的保留程度；主动定位，评估上下文重注是否能缓解对话漂移。该基准包含来自50份文档的1,636组对抗性验证问答对，覆盖五个专业领域，并支持对定位准确率、幻觉和响应延迟的全自动评估。在涵盖级联式、专有全双工与实时系统、以及开源权重语音到语音架构的多种系统中，我们发现有效定位能力存在显著差异。虽然级联式流水线（ASR-LLM-TTS）达到最高定位准确率，Gemini-Live和GPT-Realtime紧随其后。开源系统表现出独特故障模式，最显著的是上下文容量的突然崩溃和多轮定位衰减。更广泛而言，随着上下文和对话负载增加，定位保真度下降，故障常表现为无依据生成而非回避。我们表明，上下文定位是实现可靠全双工语音代理的关键未解决问题。

Signed Lexical Confidence for Risk-Calibrated Intent Routing
符号词汇置信度用于风险校准的意图路由
Selective intent routing allows an assistant to act on reliable predictions while deferring uncertain requests. Standard confidence scores primarily reflect the base model's representation, leaving an opportunity to incorporate complementary evidence without changing its decisions. We introduce a signed lexical gate that combines a sentence classifier's logit margin with a sparse lexical model's support for the classifier's predicted intent. By assigning positive evidence to lexical agreement and negative evidence to a lexically favored competing intent, the gate retains more information than either unsigned lexical confidence or a hard agreement rule. An independent binomial calibration stage selects an operating threshold for a specified risk target. Across ten runs on BANKING77, CLINC150, and HWU64, the proposed score reduces area under the risk-coverage curve by 15.8%, 15.1%, and 11.8% relative to a learned semantic-only gate. At a nominal 5% error target, it increases accepted coverage by 1.83 and 5.14 percentage points on BANKING77 and HWU64, while CLINC150 is already near full coverage. At a stricter 2% target, the simultaneous binomial procedure yields a nonempty policy in all 30 dataset-run combinations at the available calibration budgets. Matched controls show that the proposed feature improves average error ranking over the tested unsigned lexical-confidence feature, with dataset-dependent gains over binary agreement. The resulting two-feature gate provides a compact, interpretable confidence enhancement for risk-calibrated intent routing while preserving the base classifier's predictions.
中文翻译
选择性意图路由允许助手仅对可靠预测采取行动，而将不确定请求推迟处理。标准置信度分数主要反映基础模型的表征，为不改变其决策的前提下引入互补证据提供了机会。我们引入一种符号词汇门，结合句子分类器的logit差距与稀疏词汇模型对分类器预测意图的支持程度。通过为词汇一致赋予正证据、为词汇支持的竞争意图赋予负证据，该门保留了比无符号词汇置信度或硬性一致规则更多的信息。独立的二项式校准阶段为指定风险目标选择操作阈值。在BANKING77、CLINC150和HWU64上的十次运行中，所提分数相对于学习的仅语义门分别将风险-覆盖率曲线下面积减少15.8%、15.1%和11.8%。在名义5%错误目标下，其在BANKING77和HWU64上分别将接受覆盖率提高1.83和5.14个百分点，而CLINC150已接近全覆盖率。在更严格的2%目标下，同步二项式过程在所有30个数据集-运行组合中均产生非空策略，且在可用校准预算范围内可行。匹配对照显示，所提特征在平均错误排序上优于测试的无符号词汇置信度特征，且在不同数据集上优于二元一致。最终的双特征门为风险校准的意图路由提供了一种紧凑、可解释的置信度增强方案，同时保留了基础分类器的预测。

When a Data Artifact Isn't a Shortcut: Causal Auditing of Synthetic RLVR Corpora
当数据伪影并非捷径时：合成RLVR语料库的因果审计
Several recent pipelines build RLVR training data by masking a span of real corpus text and asking a language model to invent plausible wrong answers around it. The correct option is therefore genuine human prose; every distractor is synthetic. Correctness and provenance become entangled, and a policy could in principle learn the second instead of the first. We audit that possibility in GooseReason-0.7M. First we ask whether the asymmetry is visible at all: a classifier reading only five surface statistics (never the meaning) reaches AUROC 0.562 over 315,499 options, barely above chance. The aggregate hides something, though. Code sits at 0.416, below chance, and manual inspection explains why: code distractors turn out to be single-operator mutations of the gold answer rather than freely written alternatives, so the two classes are nearly identical by construction. Detecting a signal is not the same as showing a model uses it, so we then run an intervention. We build a paraphrase-matched control corpus, hold training-set size identical across arms, and train two policies under one fixed budget. The exploitation gap does not favour the unmodified-data arm: 0.021 against 0.027 for the control. Under our budget, in other words, a detectable artifact went unexploited. We think that dissociation, along with the domain-specific construction finding, is worth knowing for anyone curating corpora of this kind, and we release the audit as a mostly CPU-only protocol.
中文翻译
近期多个流程通过遮蔽真实语料文本的一段内容，并要求语言模型生成合理的错误答案来构建RLVR训练数据。因此，正确选项是真实的人类语料，而所有干扰项均为合成生成。正确性与来源变得纠缠不清，策略模型有可能学习到后者而非前者。我们在GooseReason-0.7M中审计了这种可能性。首先我们考察这种不对称性是否可见：仅读取五个表面统计特征（从不涉及语义）的分类器在315,499个选项上达到AUROC 0.562，略高于随机水平。然而，聚合结果掩盖了真相：代码类别的得分仅为0.416，低于随机水平，人工检查解释了原因——代码干扰项实际上是黄金答案的单操作变异，而非自由撰写的替代项，因此两类在构造上几乎完全相同。检测到信号并不等于证明模型使用了该信号，因此我们进行了干预实验。我们构建了一个同义改写匹配的对照语料库，在各组保持相同的训练集大小，并在固定预算下训练两个策略。结果表明，未修改数据组并未占据优势：0.021对比对照组的0.027。换句话说，在我们的预算条件下，一个可检测的伪影并未被实际利用。我们认为，这种分离现象以及特定领域构造的发现对于此类语料库的制作者具有重要参考价值，因此我们发布了该审计作为主要基于CPU的协议。
👤 Esther Xin 📅 2026-09-20 🔗 arXiv

Metric-Construction Coupling Inflates Measured Synthetic Dialect Recovery
度量构建耦合导致合成方言恢复测量值虚高
Korean dialect corpora are available but not redistributable: weights may be released, while reproducing training and evaluation from the underlying data cannot be. We ask how much of that supervision synthetic data recovers, and whether that recovery can be measured independently of the synthesis pipeline. We contribute KoDialectBench, 1,000 items across five regions on three axes, released as identifier hashes and scoring code so users reconstruct the items from their own licensed copy. Recovery is strongly axis-dependent: our best synthetic arm reaches 91.2% of the real-data gain on region identification but 63.7% on comprehension. On generation the answer depends on the metric: the deployed marker lexicon reports 92.3% on dialectness and 119.3% on region match, the latter exceeding the real-data reference, whereas reference-based generation reaches 72.4%. We find the marker metrics' scoring inventory is entirely contained in the inventory our transformation rules can emit. We test the effect of construction access directly with an exact-form construction-disjoint arm that withholds 20% of marker types from the rules. At exactly matched training size (8,600 examples) it reduces dialectness recovery from 91.8% to 8.1% and region-match recovery from 101.9% to 25.6% on the held-out marker inventory, while the three pipeline-independent measurements do not fall at all. A complementary evaluator sweep defines metric-construction coverage (MCC) and finds measured dialectness recovery increasing monotonically as overlap rises from MCC=0 to MCC=1. Shared construction and evaluation inventories can therefore substantially inflate estimates of synthetic-data recovery.

韩语方言语料库虽存在但不可再分发：权重可能发布，但无法从原始数据重建训练与评估过程。我们探讨合成数据能恢复多少监督信号，以及这种恢复能否独立于合成流程进行测量。我们提出了KoDialectBench，包含五个地区、三个维度共1,000个条目，以标识符哈希和评分代码形式发布，用户可基于自有授权副本重建条目。恢复效果强烈依赖于维度：最佳合成方案在区域识别上达到真实数据收益的91.2%，但在理解任务上仅为63.7%。生成任务的结果取决于度量方式：部署的标记词典在方言性上报告92.3%，在区域匹配上报告119.3%（超过真实数据基准），而基于参考的生成则达到72.4%。我们发现标记度量的评分库存完全包含在转换规则所能生成的范围内。我们通过一个精确形式的构造不相交组直接测试构造访问的影响，该组从规则中隐藏20%的标记类型。在完全匹配的训练规模（8,600个样本）下，方言性恢复从91.8%降至8.1%，区域匹配恢复从101.9%降至25.6%（针对保留的标记库存），而三个与流水线无关的测量指标则无变化。进一步的评估者扫描定义了度量-构造覆盖度（MCC），并发现随着MCC从0升至1，测得的方言性恢复呈单调上升趋势。因此，共享的构造与评估库存会显著夸大合成数据恢复的估计值。

FACET at WMT 2026 Automated Translation Quality Evaluation Task
FACET在WMT 2026自动翻译质量评估任务中的表现
Different error types in machine translation require different evidence. Whether meaning is preserved can be judged only against the source, while whether the target is well-formed, or whether it names one entity consistently, can be judged from the target alone. We present FACET, our reference-free submission to the WMT26 Automated Translation Quality Evaluation Task, which decomposes evaluation into Fluency, Accuracy, and Consistency passes and gives each pass only the context its error type requires. A single fixed model is prompted three times, and the merged error spans yield the three task outputs, error spans, quality scores, and error-free labels, with no trained components. We also submit FACET-C, which omits the Consistency pass. Without gold labels, we characterize the predictions of FACET. Its system rankings place post-edited human translation first, and the Consistency pass changes about a tenth of segment scores while leaving the ranking nearly unchanged.
中文翻译
机器翻译中的不同错误类型需要不同的证据依据。是否保留原意只能根据源文判断，而目标语是否通顺或是否一致命名同一实体，则仅需依据目标语即可判断。我们提出FACET，这是提交给WMT26自动翻译质量评估任务的无参考版本，将评估分解为流畅性、准确性和一致性三个阶段，并为每个阶段提供其所需错误类型的上下文。单一固定模型被提示三次，合并后的错误片段生成三项任务输出：错误片段、质量评分和无错误标签，且无需任何训练组件。我们还提交了FACET-C，省略了一致性阶段。在无真实标签的情况下，我们分析了FACET的预测表现。其系统排名将经过后期编辑的人工翻译排在首位，而一致性阶段仅改变了约十分之一的段落评分，整体排名基本不变。
👤 Ahrii Kim, Chanjun Park, Seong-heum Kim 📅 2026-09-08 🔗 arXiv

BudgetSchemaBench: A Budget-Swept Diagnostic for Schema Context in Text-to-SQL
BudgetSchemaBench：一种针对文本到SQL中模式上下文的预算扫描诊断工具
Data agents over structured sources must fit database schema into the model's context window. Large catalogs can span many databases and thousands of columns, so cost constraints may require choosing between table coverage and serialization detail well before the context window is full. We introduce BudgetSchemaBench, an execution-grounded diagnostic for this setting. Its construction derives relevance labels mechanically from gold SQL, without human- or LLM-authored ground truth. Using a pooled 80-database catalog, we sweep four schema-context budgets and compare three representations while keeping each retriever's table ranking fixed. A source-namespace check rejects queries that obtain the correct result from the wrong database. The evaluation covers three conditions: end-to-end retrieval; frozen-gold, in which the required tables are guaranteed; and a probe that removes those tables. For the primary solver with raw serialization, raising the budget from 2.5% to 50% of the catalog improves execution accuracy on 1,279 held-out questions by 18 percentage points under lexical retrieval but only 3 under dense retrieval; the dense retriever already finds most required tables at the smallest budget. When the required tables are removed, 94.6% of correct predictions name one of them exactly, consistent with reconstruction of absent schema from parametric knowledge. For the two main solvers in the frozen-gold condition, the three representations differ by at most 2 percentage points, and the widest paired 95% confidence interval bounds the difference within +/-4 points. We observe the same qualitative patterns with one reasoning model from a different family. When retrieval is coverage-limited, execution accuracy is more sensitive to the schema budget than to the tested serializations. The diagnostic and the code used to construct and evaluate it are publicly available.
中文翻译
数据代理在处理结构化数据源时，必须将数据库模式放入模型的上下文窗口。大型目录可能涵盖多个数据库和数千列，因此在上下文窗口未满之前，成本约束可能就需要在表覆盖范围与序列化细节之间做出权衡。我们提出了BudgetSchemaBench，这是一种基于执行结果的诊断工具，用于该场景。其构建通过黄金SQL机械生成相关性标签，无需人工或大语言模型生成的真值。使用包含80个数据库的综合目录，我们对四种模式上下文预算进行扫描，并在保持每个检索器表排名不变的前提下比较三种表示方法。源命名空间检查可排除从错误数据库获得正确结果的查询。评估涵盖三种条件：端到端检索；冻结黄金（即所需表已保证存在）；以及移除这些表的探测条件。对于主求解器采用原始序列化时，将预算从目录的2.5%提升至50%，在1,279个保留问题上，词法检索下的执行准确率提高18个百分点，而密集检索仅提高3个百分点；密集检索器在最小预算下已找到大多数所需表。当所需表被移除时，94.6%的正确预测能精确命名其中一个表，表明模型可从参数知识中重构缺失的模式信息。在冻结黄金条件下，两个主要求解器的三种表示方法差异不超过2个百分点，最宽的成对95%置信区间将差异限制在±4点以内。我们观察到来自不同家族的一个推理模型也呈现相同的定性模式。当检索受限于覆盖率时，执行准确率对模式预算的敏感度高于对测试序列化方式的敏感度。该诊断工具及其构建与评估代码已公开可用。

The First Token Is Not the Verdict: Hidden Costs of Reading LLM Judges Without Generating
首个词元并非判决：不生成情况下阅读LLM裁判结果的隐藏代价
Reading an LLM judge's verdict from the logits of its first generated token is cheap, requires no generation, and is exactly what constrained decoding and likelihood-scoring evaluation harnesses produce. We show that this readout distorts position bias in one direction: it overstates it in every condition we test, so figures obtained this way behave as upper bounds. The mechanism is that judges do not always lead with a verdict token, on 12% to 49% of pairs for three Qwen3 judges and under 3% for Llama-3.1-8B and Phi-3.5-mini, and forcing a read on those pairs returns whichever response was shown first rather than a judgment. Pooled over the 924 pairs where a judge did not commit, the forced read flips on 89.7% of them when the responses are swapped, against 47.5% read after generation (paired difference +0.422, 95% CI [+0.365, +0.467]). The distortion is specific to what is measured: it moves position bias by 42 points while moving judge accuracy by under one point in seven of ten conditions, so it misleads whoever audits a judge rather than whoever uses one. A second, smaller failure occurs even when the judge does lead with a verdict token, since it sometimes opens with one letter and reasons its way to the other, on 0 to 5.5% of pairs at a rate uncorrelated with compliance. We recommend reporting the rate at which a judge leads with a verdict token, which costs one forward pass and no labels, alongside any position-bias figure.
中文翻译
从LLM裁判者的第一个生成词元的logits中读取判决结果成本低廉，无需生成过程，正是约束解码和似然评分评估所利用的方式。我们证明这种读取方式会单向扭曲位置偏见：在所有测试条件下均高估了位置偏见，因此由此获得的数据表现为上限。其机制在于，裁判者并不总以判决词元开头，在三个Qwen3裁判者中，有12%至49%的配对未以判决开头；而Llama-3.1-8B和Phi-3.5-mini则低于3%。强制读取这些配对时，返回的是最先展示的回答而非判断。在924个裁判者未作出承诺的配对中，强制读取在响应交换后翻转了89.7%的情况，而生成后的读取仅翻转47.5%（成对差异+0.422，95%置信区间[+0.365, +0.467]）。这种扭曲是特定于测量内容的：它使位置偏见变动42个百分点，而裁判准确率在十种条件中的七种下变动不足1个百分点，因此误导的是审计者而非使用者。第二个较小的失败发生在裁判者确实以判决词元开头的情况下，因为有时会先以一个字母开头，再逐步推理出完整答案，这种情况在配对中占比0%至5.5%，且与合规性无关。我们建议报告裁判者以判决词元开头的比例，这只需一次前向传播且无需标注，应与任何位置偏见数值一同报告。

SCM-based Fairness and Faithful Explainability for Legal Document Classification
基于SCM的法律文档分类公平性与忠实可解释性
Transformer models such as LegalBERT are increasingly used in legal decision support, raising concerns about both fairness and the transparency of model explanations. These properties are usually evaluated separately, leaving open whether a debiasing intervention that changes fairness also changes how faithfully explanations reflect model reasoning. This study investigates that relationship on the ECtHR alleged-violations corpus from LexGLUE. It compares a LegalBERT baseline with a fairness-regularized variant that penalizes stereotypical warmth and competence representations during fine-tuning. The evaluation covers predictive performance, demographic fairness, and SHAP explanation faithfulness across five random seeds. At the performance-optimal regularization strength, the intervention does not reduce demographic disparity. This null result holds across two fairness definitions and a conventional word-pair control on the gender axis. Classification performance is largely unchanged. However, the intervention consistently degrades explanation sufficiency across all five seeds and three thresholds. A shuffled-pair control reproduces this degradation while leaving performance and fairness unchanged, indicating that the effect arises from contrastive representational regularization rather than specifically from the warmth and competence structure. The results demonstrate a dissociation between fairness and explanation faithfulness: changes in explanation behavior do not necessarily indicate changes in fairness, and fairness must therefore be evaluated directly.
中文翻译
Transformer模型如LegalBERT在法律决策支持中日益广泛应用，引发了对公平性和模型解释透明度的双重关注。这些属性通常被分别评估，因而无法确定去偏干预是否同时改变了解释的忠实度。本研究在LexGLUE的ECtHR涉嫌侵权语料库上探讨这一关系。对比了LegalBERT基线模型与一个在微调过程中惩罚刻板印象温暖度与能力感表示的公平性正则化变体。评估涵盖预测性能、人口统计公平性以及SHAP解释的忠实度，共五次随机种子。在性能最优的正则化强度下，干预并未降低人口统计差异。这一零结果在两种公平性定义及性别轴上的常规词对控制中均成立。分类性能基本不变。然而，干预在所有五个种子和三个阈值下一致降低了解释充分性。打乱配对控制重现了这一下降，同时保持性能与公平性不变，表明该效应源于对比表示正则化，而非温暖度与能力结构本身。结果表明公平性与解释忠实性之间存在分离：解释行为的变化不一定反映公平性的变化，因此公平性必须直接评估。

On-Device Named-Entity Recognition: A Deployability Study of Accuracy, Cost, Reliability, and Confidence
设备端命名实体识别：准确率、成本、可靠性与置信度的可部署性研究
Named-entity recognition (NER) is increasingly wanted on-device (no API, low latency, data kept local). The practitioner's question is not the leaderboard but which model is deployable, how to evaluate it without human annotation, and whether its confidence can be trusted. We answer these jointly. We place nine systems across three paradigms and 13 M to 8 B parameters: a classical tagger (spaCy), bidirectional-encoder specialists (GLiNER, 166 to 460 M), and generative LLMs run locally (Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B/4B-Instruct, DeepSeek-R1-1.5B/8B), on three datasets of differing character, and report accuracy plus two axes the literature omits: latency and output validity. Because our corpus (RSS-News) had no gold, we built silver gold from a cross-family LLM judge panel, then measured its fidelity against benchmark gold and a full human re-validation of the corpus (strict F1 0.95, an upper bound since the human gold was silver-seeded); gold provenance flips the paradigm ranking, moving from LLM-authored silver to human gold raises every encoder and lowers every generative model. On accuracy alone a 4 B instruct LLM is competitive (it leads on clean newswire), so the encoder's case is deployability: it matches or slightly trails at one-ninth to one-twenty-fourth the size, at millisecond-to-second latency, with zero malformed output, while the smallest generative models emit up to 27% invalid output on long inputs, a failure fixed by scale, not output budget. We then characterize GLiNER's per-span confidence: it ranks correctness well (AUROC 0.76 to 0.86) but is overconfident (ECE 0.24 to 0.47, halved by temperature scaling); thresholding gives a small honest out-of-sample F1 gain; an all-local small-to-large cascade gives a modest, corpus-dependent gain over cost-matched random routing; and confidence tracks correctness but not novelty. Every number recomputes offline from per-span records.

命名实体识别（NER）在设备端的应用日益受到关注（无需API、低延迟、数据本地化）。实践者关心的不是排行榜，而是哪个模型具备可部署性、如何在无人工标注的情况下评估模型，以及其置信度是否可信。我们对此进行了综合回答。我们在三种范式下部署了九个系统，参数规模从13M到8B不等：经典标注器（spaCy）、双向编码器专家（GLiNER，166M至460M），以及本地运行的生成式大语言模型（Qwen3-0.6B/1.7B/4B-Instruct，DeepSeek-R1-1.5B/8B），在三个不同特征的数据集上进行测试，并报告了准确率以及文献中常被忽略的两个维度：延迟和输出有效性。由于我们的语料库（RSS-News）没有真实标签，我们构建了银色标签，通过跨家族大语言模型评委小组生成，再将其与基准真实标签及全量人工复核结果对比（严格F1达0.95，为上限，因人工标签基于银色种子生成）；真实标签来源的改变颠覆了范式排名，从LLM生成的银色标签转向人工真实标签后，所有编码器表现提升，而所有生成模型表现下降。仅看准确率，一个4B指令型LLM已具竞争力（在干净新闻语料上领先），因此编码器的优势在于可部署性：其规模仅为前者的1/9至1/24，延迟为毫秒至秒级，且输出完全有效；而最小的生成模型在长输入下会产生高达27%的无效输出，这一问题需通过模型规模扩大而非输出预算调整来解决。随后我们分析了GLiNER的逐片段置信度：其对正确性的排序表现良好（AUROC 0.76至0.86），但存在过度自信问题（ECE 0.24至0.47，经温度缩放后减半）；阈值设定带来小幅诚实的样本外F1提升；全本地的小到大级联策略在成本匹配条件下带来适度且依赖语料的增益；置信度与正确性相关，但与新颖性无关。所有数值均基于逐片段记录离线重新计算。

ScholarCatalyst: A Benchmark for Retrieving Papers That Inspire New Research
ScholarCatalyst：检索启发新研究论文的基准测试
What makes great scientists great? Even as AI systems start to make progress on open problems, scientists remain far ahead of them at sensing which prior idea, buried in an ever-growing archive of research, a new problem needs. To study this skill, we draw on researchers who know firsthand which earlier work advanced their completed projects, with papers serving as pointers to the ideas within. Using our automated pipeline that makes author annotation scalable, we build ScholarCatalyst by having 184 lead authors of 207 recent computer science papers label which candidates did or could have advanced their project, each with a detailed rationale. We introduce a retrieval task with author-provided judgments: given an initial research question, retrieve these papers from only the literature available when the project began. Agentic search does no better than embedding retrieval (0.42 vs. 0.48 Recall@20) despite calling that same retriever as a tool. Even an agent built on Claude Fable 5.1, which may have seen the completed papers during training, reaches only 0.51 R@20. These results highlight the need for new training recipes that equip models with expert intuition for searching broad corpora. We envision ScholarCatalyst as a step toward scientific agents that can take a half-formed idea and point to the prior research it needs.
中文翻译
伟大的科学家伟大之处何在？尽管AI系统在解决开放性问题上取得进展，科学家在感知哪些先前研究思想——即使深埋于不断增长的研究档案中——对新问题至关重要方面，仍远超AI。为研究这一能力，我们基于亲身经历的科研人员，他们清楚地知道哪些早期工作推动了自己已完成的项目，论文作为其中思想的指针。通过我们自动化的可扩展作者标注流程，我们构建了ScholarCatalyst，让207篇计算机科学论文的184位第一作者标注候选论文是否曾或可能推进其项目，并附有详细理由。我们提出一项检索任务，由作者提供判断：给定一个初始研究问题，从项目启动时可用的文献中检索出这些论文。尽管代理搜索调用了同一检索器作为工具，其表现并未优于嵌入检索（0.42 vs. 0.48 Recall@20）；甚至基于Claude Fable 5.1构建的代理，即便在训练中见过完成的论文，也仅达到0.51 R@20。这些结果凸显了需要新的训练方法，以赋予模型在广泛语料库中搜索的专家直觉。我们设想ScholarCatalyst是迈向科学智能体的重要一步，这类智能体能接收一个未成熟的想法，并指出其所需的相关前期研究。

Mingbird: A Local-First Agent Harness Enabling Small Open Models to Complete Real Tasks
Mingbird：一种本地优先的智能体 harness，使小型开源模型能够完成真实任务
Small open-weight models (2-9B) run on ordinary laptops, but under cloud-scale agent harnesses they rarely complete real tasks: tool prefill overflows the context, self-correction diverges, tool demonstrations loop, and tasks are silently abandoned. We present evidence, from a controlled single-machine comparison and one third-party benchmark, that a substantial share of these failures is attributable to the harness rather than the model. We introduce Mingbird, a local-first agent harness for Windows and Ollama whose ten mechanisms compensate point-by-point for small-model failure forms, three of them representative: a byte-level net-zero prefill budget, a finish gate that re-reads the task before accepting completion, and signature-level loop detection. On LRAB, a controlled comparison holding machine, models, budgets, and scoring fixed (4 harnesses $\times$ 4 open models (2B-35B) $\times$ 18 real tasks, deterministic artifact scoring), Mingbird reaches 0.886 overall against 0.631 (goose), 0.479 (opencode), and 0.405 (agent-mini), with all 288 cells published; on $τ^2$-bench (278 tasks, three arms, one protocol) it totals 0.856 against 0.791 and 0.737; and a frontier-model probe on the same 18 tasks spans 0.997 to 0.478 across harnesses, with well-formed scaffolds staying within 0.072 of each other. A leave-one-mechanism-out ablation is reported as directional only: same-night replications of the same arm move its mean by up to 0.069, the size of every nominal single-trial delta, and the one batch-matched comparison (full mechanism stack versus text re-read alone) gives the executable completion guards a paired +0.10 across three replications. The evidence carries stated limits: a self-built benchmark, a single machine, and single-trial scoring.
中文翻译
小型开源模型（2-9B参数）可在普通笔记本电脑上运行，但在云规模的智能体harness下却很少能成功完成真实任务：工具预填充溢出上下文、自我修正发散、工具演示循环、任务被无声放弃。我们通过受控单机对比和第三方基准测试提供了证据，表明这些失败中的相当一部分归因于harness本身而非模型。我们引入Mingbird，一个专为Windows和Ollama设计的本地优先智能体harness，其十个机制逐点补偿小型模型的失败模式，其中三个具有代表性：字节级净零预填充预算、在确认完成前重新读取任务的完成门机制、以及签名级循环检测。在LRAB上，一项受控比较固定机器、模型、预算和评分标准（4种harness × 4个开源模型（2B-35B） × 18个真实任务，确定性产物评分），Mingbird整体得分为0.886，而goose为0.631，opencode为0.479，agent-mini为0.405，所有288个单元数据均已公开；在τ²-bench上（278个任务，三臂，一种协议），Mingbird总得分为0.856，高于0.791和0.737；在同一组18个任务上的前沿模型探测显示，各harness得分在0.997至0.478之间波动，而结构良好的支架得分彼此相差不超过0.072。遗漏单个机制的消融实验仅具方向性意义：同晚复现相同臂的平均值变动最大达0.069，相当于每次单次试验的名义变化量，而批次匹配比较（完整机制堆栈 vs 仅文本重读）在三次复现中使执行完成保护机制获得+0.10的配对提升。该证据存在明确限制：自建基准、单台机器、单次评分。

A Safe Prototype Is Not a Safety Direction: Reference Dependence and Prompt Confounds in Response-Safety Embeddings
安全原型并非安全方向：响应安全性嵌入中的参考依赖与提示混淆
Can response safety be scored by cosine similarity to the mean embedding of known-safe responses? A recent sleeper-agent detector proposes exactly this score, yet the raw positive-centroid rule is not identified: positive observations locate the safe class relative to an encoder origin, but do not determine which direction separates safe from unsafe responses. We audit the rule on two prompt-controlled, human-labeled corpora and one auxiliary jury-labeled source control, using four frozen encoders and prompt-grouped splits. On the human-labeled corpora the safe prototype reaches ROC-AUC 0.457-0.545, with two cells significantly below chance and one above, while an explicit safe-minus-unsafe reference reaches 0.588-0.738 on the same embeddings; on the jury control the prototype is inverted (0.358-0.405) and the reference reaches 0.754-0.793. At validation-calibrated 5% false-safe thresholds, the reference accepts more safe responses on PKU-SafeRLHF (0.153-0.263 versus 0.039-0.061 across encoders) and Aegis (0.189-0.291 versus 0.004-0.045), but not reliably on BeaverTails. A fully unlabeled held-out reference recovers part to most of the referenced ranking, much less when only 5% of the pool is unsafe, whereas 80-634 labeled unsafe responses recover most of it. Prompt-only ablations show that prompt-label composition can inflate uncontrolled evaluations. This is a bounded result about a raw positive centroid, not all one-class methods or safety-specialized guards. A class mean is a location, not necessarily a safety direction; a declared reference with enough unsafe mass identifies orientation.

能否通过与已知安全响应均值嵌入的余弦相似度来评分响应的安全性？一个近期的“睡眠代理”检测器正是提出了这一评分方法，然而原始的正类中心规则并未被明确识别：正样本仅定位了相对于编码器原点的安全类别，但并不能确定哪个方向能区分安全与不安全响应。我们在两个提示控制的人工标注语料库和一个辅助的评审员标注源控制数据集上审计了该规则，使用四个冻结的编码器和按提示分组的划分方式。在人工标注语料库上，安全原型的ROC-AUC为0.457–0.545，其中两个单元显著低于随机水平，一个高于随机水平；而显式的安全减去不安全参考则在同一嵌入上达到0.588–0.738；在评审员控制数据集上，原型表现反转（0.358–0.405），而参考则达到0.754–0.793。在经过验证校准的5%假安全阈值下，参考方法在PKU-SafeRLHF（0.153–0.263 vs. 0.039–0.061）和Aegis（0.189–0.291 vs. 0.004–0.045）上接受更多安全响应，但在BeaverTails上并不稳定。一个完全无标签的保留参考恢复了部分甚至大部分参考排序，当池中仅有5%不安全样本时恢复效果较差，而80–634个标注的不安全样本则能恢复大部分性能。仅提示的消融实验表明，提示-标签组合可能夸大不受控评估的结果。这是一个关于原始正类中心的有限结论，而非所有单类方法或专门的安全防护机制。类均值是一个位置，不一定是安全方向；一个具有足够不安全质量的声明参考才能确定方向。
👤 Sahil Kadadekar 📅 2026-10-01 🔗 arXiv
